# Demo dataset credits

Generated from `manifest.json` by `fetch.ts`; do not edit by hand.

All 1727 items are by **Kent Wang** via [Wikimedia Commons](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/User:Kent_Wang), each under its own licence: 1062 under [CC BY 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/), 665 under [CC BY-SA 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/). Every item is modified: photos are resized copies, some lost their GPS for the demo, some are presented as another (fictional) user's photos in a shared album, and the videos are pan/zoom clips made from photos. The licensors provide the material as-is, without warranties (see the licence texts).
